# Review register

Reviewed on **2026-10-23** against project commit
[`c36a0581190a4d08b59c9a19b69ea728b03da5c3`](https://github.com/UnknownAlienHuman/WoW-Dev-Framework/commit/c36a0581190a4d08b59c9a19b69ea728b03da5c3).
The first documentation rewrite was rechecked against the old documents and
actual code. This register keeps the evidence, useful ideas and unresolved
questions visible. [AUDIT.md](AUDIT.md) explains the findings;
[REFACTOR_QUEUE.md](REFACTOR_QUEUE.md) remains the implementation order.

## How to read the status

- **Document `read`:** the full baseline document was read and its relevant
  ideas extracted. This does not verify its claims about implementation.
- **Document `partial`:** only the stated portion was inspected.
- **Code `traced`:** the retained requirement, or the actual caller boundary for
  a retired mechanism, was followed through its listed producer and reader.
  The conclusion can be a defect or missing implementation. It does not claim
  every historical feature was delivered or every function was audited.
- **Code `partial`:** selected branches/owners were inspected; the full
  behavior described by the document was not established.
- **Code `not_checked`:** implementation was not traced for that document.
  Any code references in that row are navigation, not verification evidence.
- **Code `not_applicable`:** contributor policy, notices, descriptive fixtures
  or retired governance without a retained implementation claim in that row.
  An active API is not assigned this status merely because it will be removed.

No tests, addon executions, editor sessions or performance measurements were
run for this review. The owner's instruction defers tests until functional
completion. The supplied Claude run counts remain **reported**, without an
independent rerun or raw run log. Compilation/CI evidence has a different scope.

The detailed inventory covers **all 329 baseline Markdown files, all read**:
**287 traced source rows, 42 not applicable, no partial or unchecked rows**.
The previously partial `crates/wow-service/e3/native-primary-pack/artifact.md`
was fully decoded as inert data: 302,654 bytes, 14 records, 3,165 scalar leaves
and 282 distinct nested keys. Its `include_bytes` consumer in
[`context/tests/build.rs`](../crates/wow-service/src/context/tests/build.rs)
was read, not run. The fixture remains unchanged and is not a product spec.
Code-status counts apply to overlapping document requirements, not a percentage
of the Rust codebase or proof that implementation is complete.

Disposition concerns the old specification: **keep** its useful behavior,
**simplify** that behavior and remove unnecessary mechanisms, **retire** the
obsolete scope, or preserve **data/attribution** where still required. It does
not mean keep every old file or implement every historic feature. The current
callers of retired publication/pack mechanisms are recorded so removal preserves
the necessary behavior without restoring that old platform.

## Source chains and conclusions

All rows below are source/protocol inspection unless explicitly marked open.
The code remains unchanged by this documentation correction.

| Area | Evidence followed | Conclusion / next implementation change |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Main/Library/STD | `wow-emmy/src/references.rs`, `syntax/analyzer.rs`; pinned upstream `EmmyLuaAnalysis::new` and `init_std_lib` | Shared Main+Library exists. STD initialization is absent; load it once in the same owner and retain separate WoW availability semantics. |
| Diagnostics/configuration | `syntax/analyzer.rs`, `syntax.rs`, `flow.rs`; Service LSP `diagnostics.rs` | Four-code filter, lost messages, forced normalized severity and separate fixed settings remain. Pass the configured upstream result through the real CLI/LSP reader. |
| Check selection | `wow-rules/src/engine.rs` → `wow-service/src/local/projection.rs` | Both providers run before filtering. Select before execution; one optional rule must not invalidate unrelated checking. |
| Check completeness | `local/projection.rs::components` → `service.rs::derive_status` → `local/response.rs` → CLI `output.rs` | Component state ignores requested rules; native annotation input is always degraded. Successful native checks therefore remain partial. Derive completeness from requested work and preserve its real blockers. |
| API receiver binding | `references.rs::reference_resolution` → rule `api_lookup_key_parts`; pinned Emmy local-name/type/member inference | A local with an unrelated concrete class type and API-like namespace spelling can reach an API-absence finding when its missing member passes the other gates. The conditional false-positive path is source-traced, not executed; plain table receivers remain conservative. |
| Secret producer uncertainty | Member-call facts → `flow.rs::direct_member_initializer` → `engine.rs::evaluate_secret` | A coherent non-resolved candidate with matching restriction metadata reaches the fact-graph error branch. Separate semantic uncertainty from invalid identities; selected-corpus occurrence is not executed. Normal producer identity mismatches were not established. |
| Exact API absence | `native_view.rs` corpus closure → `deterministic_view.rs::ReferenceView::lookup` → rule `exact_lookup` and decision branch | Complete-domain absence covers the selected APIDocumentation manifest, not all runtime globals/UI exports. Keep exact-key conflicts and positive facts under partial broader coverage. These guards do not repair a misidentified receiver. |
| LSP project input | `wow-project/src/overlay/state.rs` → Service frontend | One-file overlay analysis omits project/Library context. Reuse the configured project session and unsaved text. |
| LSP versions | `change_document` → `frontend_document_change`; official pinned LSP definition | `current + 1` rejects valid increasing versions with gaps and marks resynchronization required. Preserve text guards while accepting monotonic client versions. |
| LSP empty references | `local_references` ResolvedLocal → Service locations → App `result_response` | An evaluated empty reference list with includeDeclaration=false becomes -32001. Return the standard empty result while distinguishing NotEvaluated/failed input. Definition's None currently comes from unevaluated/parse-failed input, not a proven successful no-match branch. |
| MCP metadata/notifications | `McpServer::handle_message`, `initialize_params`, method parsers | Unknown notifications close the session. params._meta is accepted only empty at initialization and rejected by several methods. Keep frame/depth bounds while fixing protocol extensibility; ordinary check/search tools are still absent. |
| Stride groups | Gethe `GetMetaPowerInfo` declaration and `MetaPowerTooltipHelper`; `native/fields.rs::convert` → `validate_fields` → native issue emission | Repeated triples are reduced to independent variadics and the callable is omitted. Preserve group positions and use a supported, explicitly lossy lowering where exact typing is unavailable. |
| Nilable contents | Gethe EquipmentManager and AddOnProfiler descriptors → `native_model::field` → `native/fields.rs` → `ketho/callbacks.rs::field_type` | Contents nilability is absent from the typed projection. Keep it separate from container nilability and zero-result alternatives. |
| Emmy annotation forms | Pinned `grammar/doc/types.rs`, `analyzer/doc/infer_type.rs`, return guide | Nullable element unions and homogeneous variadic returns have parser/type-model support. An exact repeating heterogeneous tuple form was not established. No consumer execution is claimed. |
| API environment | Gethe FrameScript member `SecureOnly` inside an `All` system → native model and emitter filtering | Member environment does not participate in filtering. Flavor, game/glue load context and API execution context must stay separate. |
| Other API metadata | Current Blizzard descriptors → `native_model.rs`, `native_view.rs`, annotation sidecars | `MayReturnNothing`, mixins and restriction semantics are incomplete. The narrow first-return secret/predicate projection already exists and should be reused. |
| Metadata reader | Native `metadata_sidecars` → `source-report.json` → `local/native_artifact/wire.rs::LibraryBinding` | Sidecars contain field names/source links; values remain in raw documents. This wire reader discards sidecars with `IgnoredAny`. Emit and consume the small typed handoff needed by selected checks. |
| Corrections | `native_corrections` guards → annotation receiver/class validation | Correction acceptance and successful declaration emission are separate outcomes. A later name collision can block an accepted rename; an unchanged accepted replacement is not itself a bug. |
| Alias and type handoff | Builder `catalogs.rs` → alias projection/`aliases/callable.rs`; native type-resolution overlay and `native/type_declarations.rs` | Alias closure is transitive; declaration sidecars follow direct callable/alias atoms, while Emmy sees the whole registered Library. No consumer requirement for recursive sidecar class-field closure was found. Current workflow selects six catalogs, not a full-tree drift gate. |
| Raw/typed normalization | `native.rs` → `native_model.rs` → native signatures/type candidates | Duplicate-safe objects, ordered dense arrays, explicit false/nil/defaults and unresolved raw forms are retained or rejected explicitly. Absent collections become empty typed vectors; traced consumers do not establish a required distinction. Raw values remain available. |
| Source-map consumer | Annotation `navigation` → `local/native_library_input.rs` → `local/native_target.rs`; artifact wire reader | Exact navigation APIs exist. The old path regenerates/checks maps; API-target linking accepts a unique Blizzard declaration, not external-alias mappings. Direct ordinary Library map loading remains to be wired. |
| TOC selection | Project `load/toc.rs`; builder `io.rs` | Preserve order, conditions and dependency ambiguity. Cosmetic metadata currently damages completeness; Interface mismatch can reject useful analysis; builder duplicates selection logic. |
| Actual dependency spelling | Gethe FrameXMLBase TOC → `toc.rs::parse` → `package_closure.rs::project_metadata` | `Dep` is unknown in both key tables and produces no dependency edge. Share one finite reviewed table; preserve the conditional dependency. |
| Acquisition locality | `package_closure/acquisition.rs` and `Loader::expand` → Service `disk_input::acquisition_error` | Old acquisition reads unused variants/packages. Malformed included XML aborts the whole load and loses source fields. Select only relevant closure; retain located failed units and independent diagnostics. |
| XML handler context | XML index/source mapping → `xml_lua.rs::prepare` and semantic state | Virtual units and exact maps exist. Implicit callback receiver/parameters remain incomplete; syntax analysis alone does not supply them. |
| XML-only Main | `load/mod.rs` empty `loader.files` guard → package `into_namespaced_main` → `inventory.rs::build` | Physical-empty Main is refused before virtual units can supply Lua. Gethe ChatBubble is a real XML-only inline-body shape. Admit physical/virtual units without a dummy file; no original donor command was executed. |
| Package XML and mapped text | `xml_lua::prepare`/`collect_packages` → `analyzer.rs` shared session; `xml_index/inline.rs` and `map_virtual_range` | Reachable/conditional package inline units are analyzed after admission. Preserve entity/newline mappings, CDATA/comment gaps and exact captured-text checks. Partial labels do not prove this pass is absent. |
| Addon chunk arguments | Package paths → `analyzer.rs`; pinned Emmy ellipsis/call/table inference; selected Gethe/Wowless contexts | Path namespacing is not chunk context. The mapped helper design preserves ordinary per-addon private-table identity and lexical scopes, with synthetic origins excluded from user facts. Handler/inline/secure/suppressed contexts are distinct; the adapter remains to implement and exercise. |
| XML completeness | `load/xml.rs::parse` → `Loader::issue` → `external_files_complete` / package closure → Service health | Stale `InlineLuaNotAnalyzed` blocks legacy closure even for extracted/analyzed bodies. It does **not** by itself block the independent native XML census. |
| Live selected-load updates | `wow-service/src/live_project/operations.rs::update` | This live route rejects TOC/package-selected configurations before updating. Connect selection/context rebuilding; fresh construction and lower-level update mechanisms are not absent. |
| Package XML identity | Configuration → XML analysis/binding report IDs → analyzer/final snapshot → replay recapture | Package configuration omits standalone XML constants, but final IDs bind actual adapter versions and replay checks them. Include versions in config/cache keys too; no stale final result was established. |
| Failure/current state | Project update/failure ownership and generation checks | An old immutable snapshot can remain after failure without being a new current result. No blanket stale-publication defect was established; preserve generation rejection and explicit failure state. |
| Coordinate conversion | Pinned Emmy `LineIndex`/`LuaDocument` → `syntax/coordinates.rs` → Project `overlay/coordinates.rs` → Service LSP locations | Emmy range columns are Unicode scalars; the adapter converts to UTF-8 bytes before negotiated UTF-8/UTF-16 output. CRLF/final-line and invalid-boundary handling are source-traced; no editor run. |
| Local navigation | Emmy `navigation.rs`/`local_references.rs` → Service LSP definition/references | Actual same-file lexical declaration/reference identities and snapshot-bound positions are reused. Separate one-file constructors remain; this is not whole-project language-server coverage. |
| Diagnostic presentation | `local/projection.rs::check_context` → `service.rs` → `presentation::build` | Raw findings are retained, but the local route supplies no causal relations. Do not claim automatic root-cause folding from the presence of the graph; keep evidence-based related diagnostics in the simpler result. |
| Git source reads | Builder `io.rs` | Two blocking Git calls per selected file remain. Batch enumeration/blob reads and cancel/kill/reap owned child I/O. |
| Store/search | Store search schema → Reference documentation rows → `Service::durable_query_shard`; separate Project replay | Nested SQLite/hex/JSON remains. Reference documentation can be indexed; Lua bodies are not. Durable shard reads already avoid analyzer hydration. Four pagination families self-measure repeatedly. Preserve useful SQL/selection while replacing the storage shape. |
| Neighbor batches | Context map construction → Graph `neighbors_batch::collect_batch` | One physical edge scan dispatches by root indexes; Q×E is conservative logical reservation, not measured repeated scans. Preserve batching or use indexed adjacency in direct storage. |
| SQL cancellation | `SearchStoreReader` exact/literal queries and validation | No stop/deadline or SQLite interruption hook; outer checkpoints/row caps do not bound a running statement. Add real interruption and bounded lock wait; no latency benchmark was performed. |
| Error fidelity and cleanup | `session::map_syntax_error`/`map_local_flow_error`; Context `with_checked_context_read` | Most native failure detail is collapsed; close failure can replace an earlier semantic error. Preserve primary cause/location and secondary cleanup error without the old taxonomy/lease platform. |
| Source context | Search selection → Context inspect/build; source handle/excerpt validation → `render.rs` | Exact-source checks are useful; escaping every ASCII punctuation mark is not. Preserve readable excerpts, outline/direct-neighbor queries, scope and empty/partial/truncated distinctions. |
| Active WoW rules | `wow-rules/src/lib.rs` and current engine | Only API existence and the narrow local secret operation are active providers. Event-handler validation and broad callback/secret summaries remain planned. |
| Shared input limits | `wow-core::library_limits`, builder/output, Library readers and replay/graph limits | Acquisition ceilings align at 4,096 files, 8 MiB/file and 64 MiB total. The traced downstream replay/graph caps are distinct; maximum-size end-to-end acceptance remains unexecuted. |
| Cancellation | Framework file/conversion checkpoints; pinned synchronous Emmy diagnostics; builder child reads | Cancellation exists between stages/files. In-progress synchronous analysis and blocking child I/O have narrower limits; do not call all cancellation absent or fully preemptive. |
| Decode/admission | Shared source-map geometry and reported #232 rejection-phase cases | Successful deserialization is not semantic admission. Do not remove actual owner/path/span validation with obsolete envelopes. No final acceptance of invalid input was established by those reported phase failures. |
| Repository checker | `xtask::run` → `repository::check` → `skill::sync` | Skill copies are already compared by check. Corrected the supplied audit and first rewrite's contrary statement; Markdown link validation remains separate. |

Paths abbreviated `wow-*` in this table mean `crates/wow-*`. Precise links and
the source-to-consumer explanation for defects are in [AUDIT.md](AUDIT.md).

## The 13 failures reported by Claude

All thirteen reported cases were inspected at source level. This does not
reproduce their failures or verify the reported run totals, failing instruction,
timing, or first bad commit. The supplied review has no raw per-test log.
The five cases it attributes to #232 distinguish decode from later owner
admission. The other eight are not uniformly disposable golden comparisons.

| Reported test | Source evidence | Consequence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `standalone_toc_xml_pair_replays_without_disk_and_rejects_archive_substitution` | [Live-project tests](../crates/wow-service/src/live_project/tests.rs): adding `obsolete_schema` can fail `serde_json::from_value(changed)?` before the intended `hydrate(...).is_err()` assertion. | Early rejection is not acceptance of a substituted archive. Assert the intended admission boundary when that retained behavior is updated. |
| `native_library_inputs_requalify_after_published_source_absent_reopen` | [Native-input tests](../crates/wow-service/src/local/platform_input/tests.rs): removing `native_library_input` is expected to fail `ProjectReplay::from_json`; semantic reconstruction is a later operation. | Preserve refusal of a mismatched Library at actual admission, not an obsolete requirement about which decode stage rejects it. |
| `selected_native_api_uses_survive_published_source_absent_reopen` | [Native-input tests](../crates/wow-service/src/local/platform_input/tests.rs): the drop/null/downgrade loop requires `from_json(...).is_err()`. | Inspect each mutation at its intended boundary; success decoding the dropped optional field alone does not establish a valid reconstructed project. |
| `retained_package_profile_binds_native_graph_and_replay_limits` | [Empty-package tests](../crates/wow-service/src/local/platform_input/tests/empty_packages.rs): missing retention limits are asserted to fail decode, while another changed-limit case is checked at hydration. | Keep validation of selected input/limits while reconciling the stage of rejection. This is not evidence that final admission accepts invalid limits. |
| `selected_primary_recipes_capture_hydrate_recapture_exactly` | [Replay tests](../crates/wow-project/tests/primary_callable_replay.rs): `refused_archive` reports “admitted” when `from_json` succeeds; it never calls `hydrate` in that helper. | The error text overstates what the helper observed. Preserve selected-recipe validation at owner admission. |
| `retained_native_pack_is_lossless_across_renderers_and_keeps_one_budget` | [Context build tests](../crates/wow-service/src/context/tests/build.rs): frozen envelope/profile/pack bytes coexist with checks for consistent renderers, cumulative work bounds, visible truncation, cancellation and failure without payload. | Retire frozen legacy bytes with their format; preserve the applicable bounded-output and failure behavior in the direct result path. |
| `retained_native_inspect_projects_exact_file_and_closure_without_losing_custody` | [Context inspect tests](../crates/wow-service/src/context/tests/inspect.rs): frozen bytes coexist with exact root/generation, shared budget, truncation and mixed/stale identity checks. | Keep relevant exact-source selection and result bounds; the old L0/L1 envelope schema is not a product requirement. |
| `current_source_absent_project_registry_preserves_frozen_context_prefix` | [Registry compatibility tests](../crates/wow-service/src/context/tests/registry_compat.rs): uses frozen generation/publication/node/pack IDs and progressive Context build after deleting source; it is not just whole-output byte equality. | Retire the old progressive pack/replay operation when its consumer is removed. Preserve only the no-substitution/source-absent read principle needed by the new stored-source lookup. |
| `retained_native_primary_map_is_exact_repeatable_and_refuses_mixed_or_missing_custody` | [Context tests](../crates/wow-service/src/context/tests.rs): frozen bytes coexist with exact root/source identity, bounded relations, privacy, cancellation and refusal of mixed/stale input. | A blanket deletion would also discard useful semantic checks. Transfer only those required by the direct navigation result. |
| `retained_project_root_enumerates_both_files_and_refuses_required_aggregate_budget` | [Context tests](../crates/wow-service/src/context/tests.rs): enumerates retained roots, checks per-root truncation and aggregate limits before comparing frozen artifacts. | Preserve truthful outline membership and truncation/bounds; retire the historical ProjectMap artifact format. |
| `selected_indexed_signatures_survive_selected_replay_and_catalog_reopen` | [Fingerprint tests](../crates/wow-service/src/local/platform_input/tests/function_fingerprints.rs): expects ordinary `open_in_namespace` to reject. [Namespace ownership](../crates/wow-service/src/live_project/namespace.rs) checks the store/catalog at open, then selected Project configuration at `read_in_namespace_checked`. | A successful store open is not a successful mismatched project read. The reported assertion is consistent with a stale boundary expectation; preserve the actual selection/configuration refusal where retained. |
| `selected_declared_headers_survive_native_replay_and_selected_durable_reopen` | [Fingerprint tests](../crates/wow-service/src/local/platform_input/tests/function_fingerprints.rs): `wrong_store` remains alive after a refused read, then the same root is opened again. [Database open](../crates/wow-store/src/project/database.rs) takes the exclusive writer lock. | The source contains a lock-lifetime conflict: close/drop the first owner before reopening. Keep mismatched-read rejection and single-writer exclusion. Attribution to the reported `OperationBusy` remains unexecuted. |
| `selected_xml_classification_reaches_service_replay_and_publication` | [XML classification tests](../crates/wow-service/src/local/platform_input/tests/xml_classification.rs): the same live `wrong_store` / second-open pattern; Service maps Store `WriterBusy` to `OperationBusy`. | This is a concrete source-level candidate for the reported busy failure, not evidence that XML classification must be deleted. Keep configuration refusal and correct owner lifetime. |

No test was edited or run. After the retained product behavior is functional,
update only its relevant checks; retired publication/Context formats need no
replacement compatibility system. Source inspection of the five Context cases
does not establish which of their many assertions failed in Claude's run.

## Ideas preserved from the old design

| Old idea | Current home | Decision |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Source revision/flavor/context and Main/Library/STD roles | [WoW behavior](WOW_LINTER.md), [Emmy](EMMYLUA_UPSTREAM.md) | Preserve distinct facts and useful typing; remove certification/profile prerequisites. |
| Ketho receiver/default/callback/type precedence and source maps | [Projection](KETHO_RUST_PORT.md) | Keep the implemented mechanisms, correct current-Blizzard gaps, and check the actual consumer syntax. |
| Ordered TOC/XML selection, dependencies and conditional reachability | [Load behavior](WOW_LINTER.md#4-toc-and-xml-form-the-load-model) | Retain source order and local uncertainty; filesystem inventory is not reachability. |
| Exact text, UTF-8 geometry and virtual source mappings | [Output/updates](WOW_LINTER.md#7-useful-output-and-updates), [frontends](FRONTENDS.md) | Preserve maps and version guards through every frontend. |
| Local declaration identity and diagnostic causes | [Emmy](EMMYLUA_UPSTREAM.md), [results](IMPLEMENTATION.md#results-and-interfaces) | Use semantic bindings, not names or message similarity; preserve raw origins without a presentation-graph prerequisite. |
| Invalidation and immutable old snapshots | [Updates](IMPLEMENTATION.md#updates) | Rebuild selection/context when needed; remove dependent facts on deletion and never silently substitute a new generation. |
| Positive/possible/unknown and scoped absence | [Typed relations](IMPLEMENTATION.md#typed-source-relationships) | Preserve distinctions in ordinary typed data; retire the generic graph/proof pipeline. |
| Outline, exact selection, direct neighborhood and source excerpts | [Navigation](IMPLEMENTATION.md#small-navigation-operations) | Retain the user operation through direct queries; retire packs, profile DSL and continuation journals. |
| Errors, cancellation, privacy and source-as-data | [Implementation](IMPLEMENTATION.md), [frontends](FRONTENDS.md), [security](../SECURITY.md) | Keep practical boundaries and meaningful errors; reduce duplicate envelopes and exhaustive taxonomies. |
| Decode versus semantic admission | [Results and interfaces](IMPLEMENTATION.md#results-and-interfaces) | Keep validation in the actual reader; use admitted typed values internally. |
| Donor reuse and attribution | [Donors](DONORS.md) and retained third-party notices | Reuse existing implementations; a donor's behavior is not automatically current Blizzard authority. |

## Implementation readiness and verification limits

The source-review backlog for the retained requirements in this inventory is
closed. The documentation defines ownership, concrete configuration, current
reuse, known defects and implementation order. Initial Lua/type checking, later
addon/XML context and later WoW rules have explicit boundaries. This is readiness
to implement the specified product; it is not a claim the product already works.
The [first slice](IMPLEMENTATION.md#first-implementation-slice) is still narrower
than the complete operation. Input discovery remains target adapter work because
current Project loading accepts explicit TOCs and a supplied package universe.

| Question | Status | Required evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Local receiver shadowing in API-absence checks | **Conditional defect path traced**, not executed | The concrete-class local path is established through pinned Emmy; the plain `{}` case does not establish it. A later permitted execution must establish observed diagnostics and cover other binding/alias forms. |
| `FallbackType` semantics | No occurrence located in the selected generated corpus; raw-only path identified | Find a relevant selected-source descriptor and its meaning before adding a new lowering requirement. |
| Repeating heterogeneous returns | No direct exact form established in inspected pinned Emmy APIs | Use a documented conservative projection with an explicit loss, or establish an upstream-supported representation. Finite tuple syntax alone is insufficient. |
| Whole Library closure and diagnostic quality on real addons | **Not executed** | After functional completion under owner policy, exercise actual generated inputs/consumer diagnostics; source inspection cannot establish this. |
| Editor sessions, all conditional loads, maximum-size inputs and performance | **Not executed** | Exercise the changed ordinary paths when permitted; do not infer their behavior from compilation or fixture counts. |
| Supplied Claude test totals and failures | **Reported execution; all 13 named sources inspected** | Keep the distinction between rejection phase and final admission; a raw run log or later permitted rerun is needed for independent execution claims and exact failing assertions. |
| Addon private-table and XML handler integration | Pinned-source design and load-kind boundaries established; **not implemented or executed** | Implement the mapped adapter in the shared owner, preserving origins and all downstream readers, then exercise cross-file identity and mapped results when permitted. |
| Package-only XML adapter-profile identity | **Source question closed:** pre-analysis omission, downstream final/replay protection traced | Carry selected versions in the simpler config/cache key. Do not claim a proven stale result or build a new profile framework. |
| Retired Store/Context/Graph contracts | Actual retained callers/removal boundaries traced; obsolete standalone obligations explicitly retired | Remove with callers while preserving the documented source/transaction/error/query behavior. No full historical platform certification or reimplementation is required. |

Each link opens the old file at the exact reviewed commit, including files
removed from the active tree. The idea describes what was learned; the current
home identifies where useful behavior now belongs. A remaining code question
does not change a completed document read into verified implementation.
